### P2.11 — stop-and-ask: composition and scenarios of the two-surface harness (2026-10-13)

Brief: plan §2.4 form, item P2.11, on `labs/2.0` @ `3d1da7b` with nothing written. Two triggers: the row's
gate runs nothing until the `integration` project of `vitest.config.mts` includes the harness, and
`tsconfig.vitest.json`, which the row does not name, has to include it for the harness to be type-checked. The
brief also reported that no ok response carries a `generation` (`src/host/WebviewHost.ts:146`), and asked how
the harness is composed, what its fake webview models, which timers it fakes and what each of the seven
scenarios asserts. Answer, verbatim; no sentinel was created, because no frozen path changes:

`````text
DECISION: Composition A1 (the real root through `activateWith`, the real `SidebarHost` and `MainPanelHost` over the harness's own fake webviews; no hand-built `HostServices`, no `TestHost` subclass; the only injected seam is `vi.spyOn(services.workflows, 'single')` for the thrown exception), fake webview B2 (a recorder plus a seven-rule client model that `expectState` reads and that P3.3 later implements for real), timers D1 (fake `setTimeout`/`clearTimeout` for the whole file, explicit `advanceTimersByTimeAsync` after a watcher fires, `vi.waitFor` everywhere else), and C1 sharpened for the missing response generation (finding C-protocol-host-7, fixed by a `P2.fix` item at the P2 phase review before P3.3; no P2.11 scenario depends on it). Config: `vitest.config.mts` integration include gains `src/test/harness/**/*.test.ts`; `tsconfig.vitest.json` include gains `src/test/harness/**/*.ts`, approved here as a file the row does not name; nothing in `eslint.config.mjs`, CI or `package.json`. Scenario `A→B→A ordering` proves the P1.7 condition (both surfaces converge) and drives the race deterministically; the selection it loses in that race is a root defect (C-state-core-6) that P2.11 records and does not fix: the fix is `P2.fix-1` at the phase review, with the harness as its oracle. Not `OWNER MUST DECIDE`.

WHY: The brief is right about the tree: `vitest.config.mts:86` includes only `src/test/integration/**/*.integration.test.ts`, so the row's gate would run nothing; `tsconfig.json:30-40` excludes `src/test` and `tsconfig.vitest.json:35-52` does not list a harness directory, so nothing would type-check it (N39, gotcha "Tests pass is not compiles"); `src/host/WebviewHost.ts:146` posts `makeOk(request.id, request.type, result)` and `src/protocol/envelope.ts:88-91` takes the optional fourth argument nobody passes, while ADR-007:29-31, ADR-008:19-24, `src/protocol/messages.ts:187` and the P3.3 tracker note all describe a response generation; `Catalog.ts:94-183` returns values, not handles, so no handler can know one. The row says "+ vitest project", which names the vitest configuration; the P1.7 and P2.10 rulings (`docs/plans/STATUS.md:492,199`) are the precedent that an answered stop-and-ask is the mechanism for the one more file (`tsconfig.vitest.json`) the row omits, and P0.11 made type-checking the tests a gate. The real root is the only composition that can show what the P1.7 ruling asked for: `refreshProjectsAndSession`, the coalesced reruns and the `session.onDidChange` trigger live in `src/extension.next.ts:136-189`, not in `src/host`, and the P2.10 handoff (`src/STATUS.md:177-184,269-272`) hands exactly that proof to P2.11. Tracing the race with B's listing held: `apply(B)` commits rev 2 and starts run 1 with `lastReconciledProject = B` (`:138`); `apply(A)` commits rev 3 and sets `rerun` (`:184-188`); run 1 then calls `session.reconcile(projects, [shared])` with B's directory list (`:148`), and `normalizeSource` (`SessionStore.ts:133-142`) turns `private` into `all` because the known set is B's; run 2 lists A's directory but `all` stays. Both surfaces receive rev 2, 3, 4 and end equal to the host, so convergence holds and the P1.7 condition is met; the selection loss is a defect of the kind C-state-core-5 named (source validated against another project's list), it is in a P2.10 file, and fixing it in P2.11 would put a root redesign inside the harness item against plan §2.1; the fix item has the harness to verify both this race and the replacement case the P2.10 note describes (`src/STATUS.md:177-184`). ADR-002:48-62 and ADR-003:17-29 give the properties the other scenarios pin; ADR-009 decisions 2 and 7 (`docs/adr/009-catalog.md:12,17`) explain why a cancelling subscriber sees `cancelled` only when the shared computation ends (`Catalog.ts:125-132,323-331`; `QueryCoordinator.ts:339-341` resolves released handles too). The installed Vitest 5.0.3 advances fake timers inside `vi.waitFor` (`node_modules/vitest/dist/chunks/index.C4tKZ0yW.js:7160`: `if (vi.isFakeTimers()) vi.advanceTimersByTime(interval)`), so D1 needs no sleeping.

DO (lettered to the brief):

1. Tracker first: paste this answer verbatim under `## Advisor decisions` in `docs/plans/STATUS.md` as `### P2.11 — stop-and-ask: composition and scenarios of the two-surface harness (2026-10-13)`, set P2.11 to `in-progress`, and add to its row: "real root over `activateWith`, both real hosts over fake webviews (recorder + seven-rule model), fake `dotnet` that edits the scratch project files; fake `setTimeout`/`clearTimeout` for the file; `vitest.config.mts` and `tsconfig.vitest.json` extended additively under the ruling of 2026-10-13; the harness records C-state-core-6 (selection lost when the selected project changes during a refresh run) and C-protocol-host-7 (no ok response carries `generation`) and fixes neither".

2. (a) `vitest.config.mts:86`: `include: ['src/test/integration/**/*.integration.test.ts', 'src/test/harness/**/*.test.ts']`, nothing else in the file (no setup file, no threshold). `tsconfig.vitest.json`: add `"src/test/harness/**/*.ts"` to `include` after the `src/test/helpers` lines and amend the comment at `:31-34` by one clause ("…; the harness of P2.11 is in `src/test/harness`"). `eslint.config.mjs`: no change; the `src/test/**/*.ts` override (`:120-127`) already applies, no fence covers `src/test`, and a second `no-restricted-imports` block is the gotcha. CI and `package.json`: no change; `npm test` is `vitest run` over all projects, `test:integration` already exists, and both CI `Test` runners (ubuntu, windows) therefore run the harness; it needs no `dotnet` and no `NUIGET_SDK_TESTS`.

3. (b) Composition, one fidelity for all seven scenarios: `createHarness()` builds a scratch tree with `createScratchDir('harness')` + `writeTree`, sets `mockWorkspace.workspaceFolders` to the scratch root, makes `mockWorkspace.findFiles` return the harness's live project list, calls `activateWith(context, { spawn: dotnet.spawn, resolveExecutable: () => 'dotnet' })`, awaits `ready`, then installs the fake timers. `resolve('sidebar')` takes the provider from `registerWebviewViewProvider.mock.calls.at(-1)[1]` and passes a fake view `{ webview, visible: true, title: undefined, onDidChangeVisibility, onDidDispose(listener) }` that keeps the dispose listener; `open('main', options?)` hands `createWebviewPanel.mockImplementationOnce` a panel whose `dispose` fires its listeners once (N58; model it on `createFakePanel` in `src/extension.next.test.ts:131-149`, written anew in the harness) and runs `nuiget.openManager`. The fake webview object has `html`, `options`, `cspSource`, `asWebviewUri`, `onDidReceiveMessage(listener)` (kept, so the fake can send) and `postMessage` (records and feeds the model). Exception after admission: in the scenario, `vi.spyOn(services.workflows, 'single').mockRejectedValueOnce(new Error('boom at https://user:secret@feed.example'))`, then an `ops/remove` request; restore the spy in the same scenario. This keeps the whole path real (`handlers.ts:347` → `operations.remove` → `queue.submit` → `run` throws → `OperationQueue.ts:113-118` derives `failed` → `awaitTicket` → `settleOperation` → response and `ops/completed`), which a throwing `run` handed to `services.queue.submit` would bypass. Queue saturation: seven `ops/install` requests, four from the sidebar and three from the main panel, interleaved, with the fake holding the first `package add`; the seventh is answered at once with `status: 'rejected'`, the ticket's `opId` (a UUID, equal to the opening chunk's and to the `ops/completed` payload's on both surfaces) and the message `the operation queue is full (1 running, 5 waiting)`; the status bar item reads `$(sync~spin) nUIget: 1 running, 5 queued`.

4. (c) Files, all new: `src/test/harness/harness.ts` (builder, driver verbs, `expectState`, `settle`), `src/test/harness/FakeWebview.ts` (recorder + model), `src/test/harness/fakeDotnet.ts` (`FakeChild`, `createFakeDotnet` with `hold`, `fail`, per-cwd `sources`, and the on-disk edits for `package add`/`package remove`), `src/test/harness/scenarios.test.ts` (one `describe('two-surface harness')`, seven `it`), `src/test/harness/STATUS.md`. Write the fake spawn a third time; do not edit `src/extension.next.test.ts` (P2.10) or `src/core/cli/DotnetRunner.test.ts` (P1.6) and do not import from either; record N59 (three unexported fake-spawn copies; consolidated when P5.12 rewrites the tests). Fake `dotnet` answers: `--version` → `10.0.401`; `nuget list source --format detailed` → the listing of `sources(cwd)`; `nuget config paths` → empty stdout, exit 0 (this is the step every `installed/get` computation runs and the one a scenario holds; the SDK probe is cached after its first answer per directory, `DotnetRunner.ts:305-309`); `package add <id> --project <p> …` → insert or replace `<PackageReference Include="<id>" Version="<v>" />` in the project file on disk, exit 0; `package remove <id> --project <p>` → delete that element, exit 0; `restore <p>` → exit 0; anything else → exit 0, empty.

5. (d) The model, seven rules, stated in `ARCHITECTURE.md` so P3.3 implements the same text (ADR-001, ADR-002, ADR-008): R1 on resolve send `session/get` and `projects/list`; apply a snapshot from a `session/get`/`session/set` response or a `session/changed` event only when its `rev` is above the last applied. R2 `projects/changed` and the `projects/list` response replace the project list. R3 when the applied snapshot's `selectedProject` becomes a real project (not `''`, not `ALL_PROJECTS`) send `installed/get` for it; on `resources/invalidated` whose `keys` contains `resourceKeys.installed(selectedProject)` send it again; a surface put in all-projects mode by the scenario (`showAllProjects(surface)`) sends `allProjects/installed` instead and re-sends it when `keys` names `projects` or any key starting `installed:`; it collects the `project` chunks by `projectPath`. R4 latest request wins per slot (`session`, `projects`, `installed`, `allProjects`): a response or chunk whose id is not the latest id issued for that slot is recorded and not applied. R5 `ops/progress` events, stream chunks by id, `ops/completed` events and terminal responses are recorded; `pending()` lists request ids without a terminal response. R6 every inbound and outbound envelope is kept verbatim (`sent`, `received`); other events (`ui/*`, `config/changed`) are recorded only. R7 `generation` on a response is recorded if present and never compared (C-protocol-host-7). `expectState({ selectedProject, selectedSource?, projects, installed, allProjects? })` first `settle()`s, then asserts for every resolved surface: its session equals `services.session.snapshot` in `rev` and values and the surfaces equal each other; its project paths equal `projects` and `(await services.catalog.projects()).map(p => p.path)`; its installed result for the selected project has `fidelity: 'provisional'` and `packages.map(p => `${p.id}@${p.version}`)` equal to the literal `installed` list; `pending()` is empty; and every request id in `sent` has exactly one response envelope in `received` (ADR-007). Literal expected lists, not a second inspector: the oracle must not share code with the subject.

6. (e) Driver, `surface: 'sidebar' | 'main'`: `resolve(surface)`, `open('main', options?)`, `dispose(surface)` (panel `dispose()` or the view's dispose listener); `selectProject(surface, projectPath): Promise<SessionSnapshot>` (`session/set`); `toggle(surface, 'includePrerelease' | 'restoreEnabled'): Promise<SessionSnapshot>` (flips the surface's applied value); `operate(surface, type, payload): { id; response: Promise<ResponseEnvelope> }` for the nine `ops/*` types, not awaited by itself; `refresh(surface): Promise<void>` (`refresh/all`); `request(surface, type, payload): { id; response }` for `installed/get` and `allProjects/installed`; `cancel(surface, id)`; `command(id, ...args)` runs a contributed command through the mock (`nuiget.sidebar.*`, `nuiget.refreshPackages`); `files.writeProject(path, xml)`, `files.deleteProject(path)`, `files.writeProps(path, xml)` write or delete on disk, update the `findFiles` list, and fire the matching mock watcher listener (`createFileSystemWatcher.mock.results[i].value.onDidChange|onDidCreate|onDidDelete.mock.calls[0][0]`, located by glob as `watcherFor` in `src/extension.next.test.ts:184-192` does) without advancing time; `settle(predicate?)` is `vi.waitFor`. Watcher events, title-bar commands and disposal are harness verbs because the scenarios need them; they are not protocol verbs and the model does not react to them.

7. (f) `vi.useFakeTimers({ toFake: ['setTimeout', 'clearTimeout'] })` installed by `createHarness` after `ready`, `vi.useRealTimers()` in `afterEach` before disposing `context.subscriptions`. Microtasks, `setImmediate` (the `FakeChild`) and file I/O stay real. Waiting: `settle()` (`vi.waitFor`, which advances the fake clock by its interval on every check); after `files.*` fires a watcher, advance explicitly with `await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(ms)` when the scenario asserts that nothing happened before the debounce (1500 ms for project and props files, `extension.next.ts:244-245`), otherwise `settle()` alone. The other fake-time consumers are `MainPanelHost.flushPending` (150 ms, `MainPanelHost.ts:112`) and the runner's timeouts (`DotnetRunner.ts:224,231,267`), which never fire in the fake unless advanced; no scenario depends on either.

8. (g) Scenarios. One `describe`, seven `it` whose titles begin with the row's phrase verbatim, optionally followed by `: ` and a clause; the gate greps the phrases. Common tree: `A/A.csproj` (`<PackageReference Include="Serilog" Version="3.0.0" />`), `B/B.csproj` (`Newtonsoft.Json 13.0.1`), both SDK-style with `net10.0`; `Cpm/Directory.Packages.props` (`ManagePackageVersionsCentrally` true, `<PackageVersion Include="Newtonsoft.Json" Version="13.0.1" />`), `Cpm/C1/C1.csproj` and `Cpm/C2/C2.csproj` each with a versionless `Newtonsoft.Json` reference. Sources: `dotnet.sources = cwd => dir A → [private, shared]; dir B → [shared]; else [shared]`.
   - `A→B→A ordering`: persisted `{ selectedProject: A, selectedSource: private }`; both surfaces resolved and showing A. `dotnet.hold(listing for dir B)`; `selectProject('sidebar', B)`; `settle` until the fake saw `nuget list source` with cwd B; `selectProject('main', A)`; release. Assert: both surfaces applied snapshots with strictly increasing `rev` (at least revs 2, 3, 4 in that order, no snapshot skipped on either), final session on both equals the host with `selectedProject: A`; both show A's installed list (`['Serilog@3.0.0']`); `selectedSource` on both equals `services.session.snapshot.selectedSource`. Do not pin the source's value: the run yields `all` (traced above); state the measured value under "Deviations" of the handoff note and in C-state-core-6 with the one-line why-comment in the test ("the host's value, not `private`: C-state-core-6; P2.fix-1 pins `private`"). Fails if a surface misses or reorders a broadcast, if the surfaces disagree, or if the installed shown is B's. The P1.7 condition (convergence) is thereby shown; write that on the P1.7 and P2.10 rows.
   - `full/scoped/watcher invalidation both orders`: full = `refresh/all` (scope `workspace`, reason `manual-refresh`), scoped = the invalidation `settleOperation` issues after an `ops/*` edit (scope `project`), watcher = `files.writeProject(A, …)` plus the project-file watcher (scope `project`, reason `file-watcher`, 1500 ms). Run the six orderings of the three pairs in one `it` with a fresh tree per ordering (or `it.each` over the six, every title still starting with the phrase). For each ordering: main resolved and showing A; `dotnet.hold('nuget config paths' for dir A)` so the first re-computation stalls; trigger X (an `ops/remove Serilog` for scoped, so the fake edits the file; `files.writeProject` adding `Foo 1.0.0` for watcher; `refresh('main')` for full); `settle` until the held call was seen; resolve the sidebar now (N8: a surface that arrives late asks `session/get`, `projects/list`, `installed/get` and the main's `received` count does not change because of it); trigger Y; advance 1500 ms when a watcher is involved; release. Assert: both surfaces' installed equals the disk state after both edits (`['Foo@1.0.0']` for scoped+watcher, `['Serilog@3.0.0', 'Foo@1.0.0']` for full+watcher, `[]` for full+scoped), exactly one terminal response per request id on both surfaces, no `installed/get` response applied whose result predates the last edit, and the number of `nuget config paths` calls for dir A after the hold is at most two (the stale computation was abandoned once, ADR-002 invariants 2 and 3). Ledger: N8 closed by this scenario.
   - `queue saturation + exception after admission`: as in step 3; after release assert six `package add` argv in `dotnet.calls` (noun-first, `--project`), six `completed` outcomes announced to both surfaces and the seventh `rejected`, the status bar `hide` called at the end, and `expectState` with the six packages. Then the spy: `ops/remove Serilog` → terminal response `status: 'failed'`, `kind: 'remove'`, `packageId: 'Serilog'`, `message` containing `boom` and not `secret`, the same `opId` on the `ops/completed` event of both surfaces; a following `ops/install` completes (the queue did not stall). Fails if the seventh is admitted, if a refusal lacks the ticket's `opId`, if the thrown error escapes the ticket, or if the message leaks the credential.
   - `project deletion mid-request`: A selected, both surfaces showing A. `dotnet.hold('nuget config paths' for dir A)`; `request('main', 'installed/get', { projectPath: A })` (held); `files.deleteProject(A)` (disk, `findFiles`, watcher `delete`); `advanceTimersByTimeAsync(1500)`; release; `settle`. Assert: the held id has exactly one terminal response, ok, with `unsupported: { reason: 'not-found' }` (the inspector never rejects, `inspector.ts:74-80`); both surfaces received `projects/changed` with `[B]` and a `session/changed` whose `selectedProject` is B (reconcile replaced the vanished project, `SessionStore.ts:127-130`); `expectState({ selectedProject: B, projects: [B], installed: ['Newtonsoft.Json@13.0.1'] })`; the late A response was not applied (R4). Do not count refresh runs. The selected source: use `shared` (in both lists) so it survives; the "source only the replacement has" case is the activation-time case of C-state-core-6 and is a root test of P2.fix-1, not a harness scenario.
   - `shared query with one subscriber cancelling`: A selected; both resolved; `dotnet.hold('nuget config paths' for dir A)` before the first `installed/get`; `request('main', 'installed/get', A)` and `request('sidebar', 'installed/get', A)` back to back; `settle` until the hold was seen; `cancel('main', id)`; release. Assert: exactly one `--version` probe and exactly one `nuget config paths` call for dir A in `dotnet.calls` (the runner dedupes the probe, the coordinator the computation, ADR-002 invariant 1); the sidebar's response is ok with `['Serilog@3.0.0']`; the main's id has exactly one terminal response, an error with `code: 'cancelled'`, and it arrives after release (ADR-002 invariant 4: the release of one subscriber does not abort the other's computation; `Catalog.ts:323-331`). Fails if the cancel aborts the shared computation (a second `config paths` call) or if the sidebar gets an error.
   - `surface disposed with pending ops`: both resolved; `dotnet.hold('package add')`; from the main panel `operate('ops/install', Foo)` (running) and `operate('ops/remove', Serilog)` (waiting), plus `request('main', 'installed/get', A)` held at `config paths`; `settle` until the hold is seen; `dispose('main')`; note the main webview's `received.length`; release both holds; `settle`. Assert: `MainPanelHost.instance` undefined and the main's `received.length` unchanged (nothing posted after disposal); both operations ran (`package add` and `package remove` argv present) and the sidebar received two `ops/completed` with `status: 'completed'` (N9 beyond `handlers.test.ts:407`: real queue, real workflows, real edits, the other surface told); the sidebar's installed converges to `['Foo@1.0.0']`; `services.queue.isBusy` false; `open('main')` again yields a fresh host whose `session/get` is answered. Fails if disposal cancels the queued ticket or if a post reaches the disposed webview.
   - `central version change affecting several projects`: tree with the CPM projects; sidebar resolved, `selectProject('sidebar', C1)`; main opened in all-projects mode (`showAllProjects('main')`); `settle` until the sidebar shows `['Newtonsoft.Json@13.0.1']` with `declaration: 'central'` and the main holds chunks for C1 and C2. `files.writeProps(Cpm/Directory.Packages.props, version 13.0.3)` (props watcher, scope `workspace`); `advanceTimersByTimeAsync(1499)` → no `resources/invalidated` yet on either surface; `advanceTimersByTimeAsync(1)`; `settle`. Assert: exactly one `resources/invalidated` per surface for the edit, `scope.kind: 'workspace'`, `reason: 'file-watcher'`, and its `keys` names both `resourceKeys.installed(C1)` and `resourceKeys.installed(C2)` (both were computed by the fan-out, ADR-002:56-59); the sidebar's installed for C1 is `['Newtonsoft.Json@13.0.3']` with `declaration: 'central'` and `definedIn` the props path; the main's chunks for C1 and C2 both show 13.0.3. Keep the row's wording `affecting`; note on P6.4's row that its gate says `affects` and that P6.4 extends or renames this scenario.

9. (h) Record `C-protocol-host-7` in the ledger: "No ok response carries `generation`: `WebviewHost.ts:146` calls `makeOk` with three arguments and `Catalog` returns values without the handle's generation, so the discard by generation of ADR-007:29-31 and ADR-008:19-24 (P3.3, `useResource`) has nothing to read. Fix: the Catalog's cacheable reads return `{ value, generation }` (or the handle's generation alongside) and the handler hands it to `makeOk`; `src/core/catalog/**`, `src/host/**`, their tests; no protocol change. Scheduled: the P2 phase review as a blocking defect (`P2.fix`), before P3.3; the fix adds the harness scenario `late response for an invalidated key is discarded by generation`." No P2.11 scenario depends on it, and no scenario asserts the field's absence. Add to the P3.3 row: "depends on C-protocol-host-7 being fixed first".

DO NOT:
- Do not hand-build `HostServices` or subclass `WebviewHost` in the harness; `src/host/handlers.test.ts` already tests that level.
- Do not use the shared mock's `createMockWebviewPanel` for the main panel (N58), and do not edit `src/test/__mocks__/vscode.ts`.
- Do not edit `src/extension.next.ts`, `src/extension.next.test.ts`, `src/host/**`, `src/core/**`, `src/protocol/**`, `src/core/contracts.ts` or `docs/adr/**` in this item; the root defect and the response generation are fix items with the harness as their oracle.

- Do not fake `setImmediate`, `queueMicrotask` or `Date` (the `FakeChild`, the coordinator and `AssetsReader` need them real).
- Do not read anything from `__fixtures__/.scratch` that the scenario did not write in the same run (plan §2.6).
`````

### P3.1 — stop-and-ask: the jest-axe dependency, the tokens and the colour gate (2026-10-13)

Brief: plan §2.4 form, item P3.1, on `labs/2.0` @ `3349d27` with nothing written. The row says "jest-axe in
`src/test/setup-frontend.ts`", but neither `jest-axe` nor its types are installed, so the item needs
`package.json` and `package-lock.json`, which the row does not name. The brief also asked for the wiring of
the matcher under Vitest's type-checking, the names and values of the tokens, the tool behind the gate "hex
colours only in `tokens.css`", whether `tokens.css` is bundled now, and the scope of the icon port. Answer,
verbatim; no sentinel, no frozen path:

`````text
DECISION: Option B, sharpened: `jest-axe` (`^11.0.0`, caret like every other entry) as the only new devDependency, no `@types/jest-axe`; the matcher is registered globally in `src/test/setup-frontend.ts`; the Vitest type augmentation (and, if `jest-axe` ships no types, an ambient module declaration of the three exports the tests use) lives in a new `src/test/jest-axe.d.ts` that `tsconfig.vitest.json` lists; `package.json`, `package-lock.json` and that `tsconfig.vitest.json` line are the item's files under this ruling. `tokens.css` is custom properties only (`--nuiget-*`), colours as `--vscode-*` aliases with `--vscode-*` fallbacks, zero hex in P3.1, HC/forced-colors/reduced-motion blocks and no light/dark blocks, imported by nothing until P3.4. All 26 icons ported, no `PackageIcon`/`BookIcon`, no barrel. The hex gate becomes two lint-as-tests in `src/test/lint/`. Not `OWNER MUST DECIDE`: the owner approved the row that names `jest-axe`, and `contributes` does not change.

WHY: The brief describes the tree accurately (checked: `src/test/setup-frontend.ts:5-18`; `tsconfig.vitest.json:26-29,46-56`; `vitest.config.mts:36-47,72-80`; `src/webview/app/icons.tsx:11-16` and 26 `export const` lines; `src/host/services.ts:103-131`; `esbuild.mjs:13-24,77-100`; `.claude/settings.json` allows exactly `Bash(npm install)` and `Bash(npm ci)`; `src/webview/shared` does not exist). The row (plan line 244) names `jest-axe` in `setup-frontend.ts`, so adding it is the item's work, not a "resource suggests it" install (`.github/copilot-instructions.md:35` is about Awesome Copilot templates); §2.1 makes a config file the item names one of its files, and the P2.11 ruling (`docs/plans/STATUS.md:1264`) is the precedent for approving one `tsconfig.vitest.json` include line here. Option A is wrong for this tree, not just redundant: `@types/jest-axe` depends on `@types/jest`, whose global `describe`/`it`/`expect` declarations would sit next to `vitest/globals` in the `tsconfig.vitest.json` program (brief point 2 is right that its `jest.Matchers` augmentation does nothing for Vitest), and the quarry's "no `tsc` impact" claim rested on tests not being type-checked, which P0.11 ended. Option C re-implements what jest-axe encodes (its default disables the `region` rule, which otherwise fails every primitive rendered in isolation) and departs from the row's text; D is unmaintained. ADR-008:33-35 wants tokens for layout, motion and typography with "theme coverage ... in one place" and "no hex outside tokens": a permission for `tokens.css`, not a requirement; every VS Code theme colour reaches a webview as a `--vscode-*` variable, so aliases cover light and dark by construction and only high contrast (`--vscode-contrastBorder`, no shadows) and `forced-colors` (system colour keywords) need their own blocks; U4, U5, U7, U9 and U11 (`docs/plans/findings-ledger.md:248-255`) are what the tokens progress, and P5.11 (plan line 284) is where they close. ADR-006:22-23 forbids a `style` attribute in rendered HTML; the 1.x icons also render `aria-hidden="false"` on titled icons (`aria-hidden={!title}`), which the port corrects. CSS needs nothing in `esbuild.mjs`: 1.x gets `dist/webview.css` from `import './App.css'` in `App.tsx:3` with no loader entry, and the 2.0 entries are P3.4's and P5.3's files, so `tokens.css` stays unbundled until P3.4 and the STATUS note says so.

DO:
1. Dependency: edit `package.json` devDependencies by hand, `"jest-axe": "^11.0.0"` after `"jsdom"`; run `npm install` (pre-approved; `npm install --save-dev` is not); check `git status --porcelain --untracked-files=all` shows only `package.json` and `package-lock.json`, and `grep -c '"node_modules/jest-axe"' package-lock.json` → 1 and `ls node_modules/@types | grep -c jest` → 0; run `npm ci` once (ADR-000:45) and `npm audit` and paste both summary lines into the STATUS note (licences: jest-axe MIT, axe-core MPL-2.0, development only; `npm run check:vsix` proves nothing ships).
2. Decide the typing shape from the installed package: `grep -n '"types"' node_modules/jest-axe/package.json; ls node_modules/jest-axe/*.d.ts`. If it ships types, `src/test/jest-axe.d.ts` holds only the Vitest augmentation. If not (expected), it also holds `declare module 'jest-axe'` with the exports the tests use: `axe(html: Element | string, options?: object): Promise<AxeResults>`, `configureAxe(options?: object): typeof axe`, `toHaveNoViolations: { toHaveNoViolations(results: AxeResults): { pass: boolean; message(): string } }`, where `AxeResults` is a self-contained interface (`violations: Array<{ id; impact?; help; helpUrl; nodes: Array<{ html; target: string[] }> }>`), no import from `axe-core` (a transitive package; importing it would be a phantom dependency). ### P3.2 — ⚑ ruling: the listbox and row markup, and the twelve primitives (2026-10-13)
`````

Brief: plan §2.4 form, item P3.2, on `labs/2.0` @ `3794311` with nothing written. It set the interaction
model's listbox with row action buttons against axe's `nested-interactive`, `aria-required-children` and
`aria-hidden-focus`, gave four options (listbox with a hidden sibling actions container, grid, plain list,
actions outside the list), and asked for the exact DOM and keyboard contract of `Listbox` and `Row`, how
virtualization reaches the DOM under ADR-006, the other ten primitives, the stylesheet layout, the tests, the
wording of the interaction-model edits and whether the item is split. Answer, verbatim; no sentinel, no frozen
path:

`````text
DECISION: Option 1, sharpened: `role="listbox"` → per row a `role="presentation"` wrapper (nothing on it but `className`, `data-*` and the measure `ref`) holding the focusable `role="option"` and a sibling actions container `aria-hidden="true"` whose `IconButton`s are `tabIndex={-1}` with `onMouseDown` prevented; roving `tabIndex`, one `Listbox` component that is always virtualized (no separate `VirtualList` export), single selection now, every action mirrored by a key the listbox handles and announced through the single `LiveStatus`; per-element numbers reach the DOM only as `--nuiget-*` custom properties through React's `style` prop (ADR-006's named mechanism), never as a layout property. One tracker row P3.2, two green commits in a fixed order (a: `Listbox`, `Row`, `IconButton`, `Badge`, `Spinner`, `LiveStatus`, `primitives.css`, tokens, §5/§8, the stylesheet lint test; b: `SectionHeader`, `EmptyState`, `Skeleton`, `ProgressBar`, `Banner`, `Sash`, the barrel, ARCHITECTURE and the gotcha rows). One stylesheet `src/webview/shared/primitives/primitives.css`, imported by every primitive module and by the barrel. `primitives/index.ts` is created in commit b. Not `OWNER MUST DECIDE`: the owner approved the row that recommends this DOM, no frozen path changes, and `contributes` is untouched.

WHY: The brief describes the tree accurately (checked: `src/webview/shared/**` has only `tokens.css`, `primitives/icons.tsx`, `icons.test.tsx`, `STATUS.md`; `src/test/setup-frontend.ts:6-10` registers the matcher; `vitest.config.mts:36-47,70-80`; `package.json:343,353`; `docs/ux/interaction-model.md:48-63,78-82,106-125`; `src/webview/sidebar/components/PackageRow.tsx:95-137` has the button inside the option; `SectionHeader.tsx:31-44` is a `role="button"` div containing action buttons; `DraggableSash.tsx:92-105` mutates `document.body.style`; `App.tsx:3039-3049` positions rows with inline layout styles). The decision is settled by axe-core 4.12.1's own code, not by its documentation: `getOwnedRoles` (`node_modules/axe-core/axe.js:27366`) drops any child that is not visible to screen readers before it looks at roles, so an `aria-hidden="true"` actions container never reaches `aria-required-children`, and it flattens a child whose role is null or presentational only when that child has no global `aria-*` attribute and no `tabindex`, otherwise the child is pushed as an owned element with a null role and fails as "unallowed"; `getMissingContext` walks through null-role parents, so the option inside the wrapper passes `aria-required-parent`; `noFocusableContentEvaluate` (`nested-interactive`) collects every focusable widget descendant and flags `tabindex < 0` as `notHidden`, so a `tabIndex={-1}` button inside the option still fails, which is why the actions must be a sibling and not merely hidden (the quarry note in brief point 3 is right about that, and this is the negative test); `focusableNotTabbableEvaluate` (`aria-hidden-focus`) reads `tabbableElements` only, so `tabIndex={-1}` buttons pass (brief point 6 is right); `option.allowedAttrs` lists `aria-posinset`/`aria-setsize`, `separator` has `requiredAttrs: ['aria-valuenow']` with `ariaRequiredAttrEvaluate` exempting only non-focusable separators (the Sash is focusable, so it carries the attribute), `progressbar` has `accessibleNameRequired: true`, `listbox` allows `aria-multiselectable` for P5. Roving `tabIndex` over `aria-activedescendant` because §5's "visible on `:focus-within`" needs real focus inside the wrapper, and because P3.5's ExTester tests can read `document.activeElement`; the unmount hazard of a virtual list is closed by a `rangeExtractor` that keeps the active index mounted (`defaultRangeExtractor` is exported, `@tanstack/virtual-core/dist/esm/index.d.ts:36`). Options 2 to 4 contradict the owner-approved model and test ids (§5 and §8 say listbox) or lose selection semantics. ADR-006:22-23 reads "styles are set through the CSSOM (React's `style` prop), never as `<style>` or a `style` attribute in rendered HTML": the `style` prop is the permitted mechanism, the icons lost it in P3.1 because they did not need it; jsdom 30 serializes custom properties (`node_modules/jsdom/lib/jsdom/living/css/CSSStyleDeclaration-impl.js:140,324`), so the test can read them back. Virtual-core's `observeElementRect` seeds from `getBoundingClientRect` and no-ops without `ResizeObserver`, and `scrollWithAdjustments` optional-calls `scrollTo`, so the real virtualizer runs under jsdom when the test stubs the rects; the 1.x tests mock `useVirtualizer` (`InstalledTab.test.tsx:7-11`), which hides exactly what this item must prove. Two commits in one row have precedent (`2dcd3c6`/`9e2ebbc` for P0.13, `b8bfb9c`/`25e9a82` for P1.3, `e080e1f`/`2f903e7` for P1.6); plan line 208 reserves two sessions for P1.1b alone, so the second commit is not a second row and not a licence for a second session.

DO:
1. Tokens (`src/webview/shared/tokens.css`, `src/test/lint/webview-tokens.test.ts`): no new token. The spinner and the indeterminate bar time themselves with `calc(var(--nuiget-duration-normal) * 5)` (1 s; 0 under reduced motion through the one existing rule). Change `--nuiget-row-height-compact` from `22px` to `24px` so the 24 px `IconButton` target fits a compact row (WCAG 2.5.8, U11); the tokens test checks names, not values. Record it under Deviations in the STATUS note and in the U11 ledger row as "compact row 24 px for WCAG 2.5.8; P3.6 may revert by value".
2. `src/webview/shared/primitives/cssVars.ts`: `export function cssVars(vars: Partial<Record<`--nuiget-${string}`, string>>): React.CSSProperties` (one cast inside). It is the only way a primitive produces a `style` prop; `src/test/lint/webview-stylesheets.test.ts` (step 11) and each component test assert that every `[style]` in rendered output matches `/^(--nuiget-[a-z0-9-]+: [^;]+;\s*)+$/`.
3. `Row` (`primitives/Row.tsx`). Props: `itemId: string` (test id `package-row-${itemId.toLowerCase()}`), `name: string`, `version?: ReactNode`, `description?: string`, `icon?: ReactNode` (comfortable only), `badges?: ReactNode` (`Badge`s), `status?: string` (e.g. "Updating", "Unresolved"), `busy?: boolean`, `disabled?: boolean`, `actions?: ReactNode` (`IconButton`s with `tabIndex={-1}` and `data-testid="row-action-<name>"`), `density: 'compact' | 'comfortable'`, and the list props the `Listbox` passes through `renderRow`: `index`, `setSize`, `active`, `selected`, `optionId`. DOM, exactly:
   `div.nuiget-row.nuiget-row--{density}[.nuiget-row--busy][role="presentation"][data-index={index}][data-testid="package-row-<id>"]` (ref = `measureElement`; `style` = `cssVars({ '--nuiget-virtual-offset': `${start}px` })` and nothing else; no `aria-*`, no `tabIndex`)
   → `div.nuiget-row-option[role="option"][id={optionId}][tabIndex={active ? 0 : -1}][aria-selected={selected}][aria-posinset={index + 1}][aria-setsize={setSize}][aria-busy="true" when busy][aria-disabled="true" when disabled][aria-labelledby=`${optionId}-title`][aria-describedby=`${optionId}-desc` when badges, description or status exist]`
     → `span.nuiget-row-title[id=…-title]` holding `span.nuiget-row-name` (name) and `span.nuiget-row-version` (version); the icon before it, `aria-hidden` or an `img` with `alt=""`
     → `span.nuiget-row-desc[id=…-desc]` holding badges, `span.nuiget-row-description`, and `span.nuiget-row-status` (visually hidden when the density has no room; always in the DOM when `status` is set)
   → `div.nuiget-row-actions[aria-hidden="true"]` with `onMouseDown={e => { e.preventDefault(); optionRef.current?.focus(); }}` (Chromium focuses a clicked button even at `tabIndex -1`, which would park focus inside an `aria-hidden` subtree); it renders `actions` or, when `busy`, one decorative `Spinner`; when `disabled` it renders nothing.
   No `aria-label` on the option (WCAG 2.5.3): the name is the visible id and version, the description carries badges, description and status. Everything is a `span`/`div`; no `button` or other widget inside the option.
4. `Listbox` (`primitives/Listbox.tsx`), generic over `T extends { id: string }`. Props: `items: readonly T[]`, `renderRow: (item: T, list: { index; setSize; active; selected; optionId }) => ReactNode` (returns a `Row`), `label: string` or `labelledBy: string` (one required), `hint: string` (required; the visually hidden sentence naming the keys, composed by the consumer, e.g. "Enter uninstalls. Shift+F10 opens the actions menu."), `testId: string`, `density`, `estimateSize?: number` (default 24 compact, 44 comfortable; note in STATUS that these numbers duplicate the two tokens because JS cannot read a token in jsdom), `overscan?: number` (default 5), `selectedId?: string | null`, `onSelect?(item)`, `onActivate?(item)`, `onSecondaryActivate?(item)`, `onDelete?(item)`, `onContextMenu?(item, anchor: { x: number; y: number })`, `busy?: boolean` (`aria-busy` on the listbox while revalidating), `empty?: ReactNode`. DOM:
   `div.nuiget-listbox-root` (flex column, `min-height: 0`) → `div.nuiget-listbox[role="listbox"][aria-label | aria-labelledby][aria-describedby={hintId}][aria-busy?][data-testid={testId}]` (the scroll element: `flex: 1; min-height: 0; overflow: auto`; no `tabIndex`) → `div.nuiget-listbox-sizer` (role-less, no aria; `style` = `cssVars({ '--nuiget-virtual-total': `${getTotalSize()}px` })`) → the rows from `renderRow` for `getVirtualItems()`; after the listbox, `span.nuiget-visually-hidden[id={hintId}]` with `hint`. With zero items the `role="listbox"` element is not rendered; instead `div.nuiget-listbox-empty[tabIndex=-1]` wraps `empty`, and the component focuses it when the list had focus-within (the screen reader reads the empty text; nothing lands on `body`). Virtualizer: `useVirtualizer({ count, getScrollElement, estimateSize: () => estimateSize, overscan, getItemKey: i => items[i].id, measureElement: (el) => el.getBoundingClientRect().height || estimateSize, rangeExtractor: range => { const set = new Set(defaultRangeExtractor(range)); if (activeIndex >= 0 && activeIndex < count) set.add(activeIndex); return [...set].sort((a, b) => a - b); } })`. State: `activeId` (internal, keyed by id so it survives reorders); `selected` is controlled by `selectedId`. Event delegation on the listbox element: `onFocus` (focusin) sets `activeId` from `closest('[data-index]')`; `onClick` on an option → `onSelect(item)`; `onContextMenu` anywhere in a row → `onContextMenu(item, { x: e.clientX, y: e.clientY })` with `preventDefault`; `onKeyDown`: ArrowDown/ArrowUp move the active row by one, Home/End to first/last (each: `preventDefault`, set active, `scrollToIndex(i, { align: 'auto' })`, focus in an effect after render), Enter → `onActivate`, Ctrl+Enter or Meta+Enter → `onSecondaryActivate`, Delete or Meta+Backspace → `onDelete`, Shift+F10 or `key === 'ContextMenu'` → `onContextMenu(item, { x: rect.left, y: rect.bottom })` of the option. Enter, Ctrl+Enter and Delete are ignored when the option has `aria-disabled="true"` (read from the DOM, so Row and Listbox cannot disagree); Space, PageUp/PageDown, type-ahead, Left/Right and Escape are not handled (they bubble; Space and `aria-multiselectable` come with P5's bulk toolbar; design `selectedId` so a `selectedIds` set is additive). Focus effect: on `[activeId, items]`, if the active id vanished, active becomes the item at `min(oldIndex, items.length - 1)`; if the listbox had focus-within, `getElementById(optionId)?.focus({ preventScroll: true })` after `scrollToIndex`.
5. `IconButton`: `button.nuiget-icon-button[type="button"][aria-label={label}][title={label}][aria-pressed?][disabled?][tabIndex?][data-testid?]` with the icon child `aria-hidden`; `label` required; `min-width`/`min-height: var(--nuiget-target-min)`. `Badge`: `span.nuiget-badge.nuiget-badge--{tone}` with `label`; when `title` is given, `span[aria-hidden="true"]{label}` + `span.nuiget-visually-hidden{title}` so the row description reads "Centrally managed", not "CPM"; no `aria-label` on a span (axe `aria-prohibited-attr`). `Spinner`: `span.nuiget-spinner` around `LoadingIcon` with `title={label}` (named → `role="img"`; unnamed → `aria-hidden`), animation `nuiget-spin calc(var(--nuiget-duration-normal) * 5) linear infinite`.
6. `LiveStatus`: `div.nuiget-visually-hidden[role="status"][aria-live="polite"][aria-atomic="true"][data-testid="status-live"]`, prop `message: string | null`, mounted from the first render of the surface (a live region announces changes only); an identical consecutive message is re-announced by clearing the text and setting it on the next animation frame. No queue and no store (P3.3): it shows the latest message. The shell does not duplicate `Banner` text into it.
7. `SectionHeader`: `div.nuiget-section-header` → `h2|h3` (`level` prop, default 2) → `button.nuiget-section-toggle[type="button"][aria-expanded][aria-controls={contentId}][data-testid=`section-header-${testKey}`]` holding `ChevronRightIcon` (aria-hidden, rotated by CSS when expanded), `span.nuiget-section-title[id]` and, when `count !== undefined`, a `Badge`; `busy` renders a decorative `Spinner` inside the button; `actions` render in a sibling `div.nuiget-section-actions` after the heading, never inside the button (1.x's `role="button"` div with buttons inside is the `nested-interactive` the item exists to remove). The title span's `id` is what a `Listbox` passes as `labelledBy`. `EmptyState`: `div.nuiget-empty[data-testid?]` → `p.nuiget-empty-text{message}` + optional `button.nuiget-button[type="button"]{action.label}`; no role, no `tabIndex` (the focus target is the Listbox's container around it). `Skeleton`: `div.nuiget-skeleton[aria-hidden="true"]` with `rows` (default 3) × `div.nuiget-skeleton-row.nuiget-skeleton-row--{density}`; no text, no role: the "Loading …" of §4 is the `LiveStatus` message. `ProgressBar`: `div.nuiget-progress[.nuiget-progress--indeterminate][role="progressbar"][aria-label={label}][aria-valuemin="0"][aria-valuemax="100"][aria-valuenow={value} only when determinate]` → `div.nuiget-progress-bar`; `style` = `cssVars({ '--nuiget-progress-value': `${value}%` })` when determinate; `label` required. `Banner`: `div.nuiget-banner.nuiget-banner--{severity}[role={severity === 'error' ? 'alert' : 'status'}][data-testid?]` → severity icon (aria-hidden), `span.nuiget-visually-hidden{`${Severity}: `}`, `span.nuiget-banner-text{children}`, optional `button.nuiget-button.nuiget-button--secondary[type="button"]{action.label}`; a second `role="status"` is acceptable here because the message must announce when it appears (WCAG 4.1.3); P3.4 tests scope `getByRole('status')` by test id. `Sash`: `div.nuiget-sash.nuiget-sash--{orientation}[role="separator"][tabIndex=0][aria-orientation][aria-valuenow={value}][aria-valuemin={min}][aria-valuemax={max}][aria-label={label}][aria-controls?][data-testid="sash"]`; props `value`, `min` (20), `max` (80), `defaultValue` (50), `step` (1), `orientation`, `label`, `onChange(value)`, `onChangeEnd?(value)`; keys: the two arrows along the axis ±step, Shift+arrow ±10·step, Home → min, End → max, Enter → `defaultValue` (keyboard twin of double-click, WCAG 2.5.7); pointer: Pointer Events with `setPointerCapture`, percent from `parentElement.getBoundingClientRect()`, clamped, `touch-action: none` and the cursor in CSS; the sash renders no `style`: the parent sets `--nuiget-split` on its layout container with `cssVars`. The hit area is widened to 24 px with a `::after` overlay around a 4 px visual.
8. `primitives.css`: class prefix `nuiget-`, BEM-lite modifiers (`--compact`, `--busy`, `--error`); `.nuiget-visually-hidden` (clip pattern) once; rows: `.nuiget-listbox-sizer { position: relative; height: var(--nuiget-virtual-total) }`, `.nuiget-row { position: absolute; inset: 0 auto auto 0; width: 100%; transform: translateY(var(--nuiget-virtual-offset)) }`, `.nuiget-row-actions { visibility: hidden }`, `.nuiget-row:hover .nuiget-row-actions, .nuiget-row:focus-within .nuiget-row-actions, .nuiget-row--busy .nuiget-row-actions { visibility: visible }`; focus ring `.nuiget-row-option:focus-visible, .nuiget-icon-button:focus-visible, .nuiget-sash:focus-visible, .nuiget-section-toggle:focus-visible { outline: 1px solid var(--nuiget-color-focus); outline-offset: -1px }` (an outline, not a shadow, so it survives forced colours; the token is already `Highlight` there); `forced-color-adjust: none` only on `.nuiget-row-option[aria-selected="true"]`, `.nuiget-badge` and `.nuiget-progress-bar`, whose tokens already map to system colours; every duration is a `var(--nuiget-duration-*)` or a `calc()` of one; no `@media (prefers-reduced-motion)`, no `!important`, no `px` font size, no colour literal. Every primitive module has `import './primitives.css'`; the barrel imports it too.
9. Tests, frontend project, one `*.test.tsx` per primitive, each ending with `has no axe violations` on its own render. Titles the gate should read: `Row`: `the option is named by id and version and described by badges and status`, `the row wrapper carries no aria attribute and no tabindex`, `the actions container is hidden from assistive technology and its buttons are not tabbable`, `clicking an action keeps focus on the option`, `a busy row is aria-busy and shows a spinner instead of its actions`, `a disabled row renders no actions`, `the only inline style is the virtual offset custom property`. `Listbox`: `renders rows as options with posinset and setsize`, `ArrowDown, ArrowUp, Home and End move the active option and the roving tabindex`, `Enter activates, Ctrl+Enter and Meta+Enter are the secondary action`, `Delete and Meta+Backspace delete the active item`, `Shift+F10 and the ContextMenu key report the active item with an anchor`, `right-click on a row reports the item`, `a disabled option ignores Enter, Ctrl+Enter and Delete but keeps focus and the context menu`, `the active row follows its id when items reorder`, `when the active item disappears focus moves to the row at its index`, `when the list empties the empty container takes focus`, `only the rows in view plus overscan are rendered, and the active row stays mounted off-screen` (stub `vi.spyOn(Element.prototype, 'getBoundingClientRect')` keyed on `this`: `[role="listbox"]` → the viewport height, `[data-index]` → the row height, installed before render; never mock `useVirtualizer`), `the only inline styles are the virtual total and offsets`, `a listbox with rows, badges and actions has no axe violations`. Negative, in `Listbox.test.tsx`, each asserting `violations.map(v => v.id)` contains the rule: `a button inside an option is a nested-interactive violation` (also with `tabindex="-1"`), `a wrapper with aria-busy between listbox and option is an aria-required-children violation`, `a tabbable button under aria-hidden is an aria-hidden-focus violation`. `IconButton`: `has the accessible name it is given`, `passes tabIndex, pressed and disabled through`. `Badge`: `reads its title to assistive technology when one is given`. `Spinner`: `is decorative without a label and an image with one`. `LiveStatus`: `is a polite atomic status region with the test id`, `re-announces an identical message`. `SectionHeader`: `is a heading with a toggle button that reports aria-expanded and aria-controls`, `actions are outside the button`, `the count renders as a badge`. `EmptyState`: `renders the sentence and the optional action`. `Skeleton`: `renders the number of rows asked and is hidden from assistive technology`. `ProgressBar`: `determinate exposes the value, indeterminate omits aria-valuenow`, `the only inline style is the progress custom property`. `Banner`: `warning and info are status, error is alert`, `the severity is spoken before the text`, `the action is a button`. `Sash`: `is a focusable separator with value, min, max and orientation`, `arrows, Shift+arrows, Home, End and Enter change the value within the clamps`, `dragging reports clamped percentages and the end value`, `renders no inline style`. Plus `primitives/composed.test.tsx`: `SectionHeader, Listbox with three rows and actions, Banner and LiveStatus together have no axe violations`.
10. Interaction model edits (`docs/ux/interaction-model.md`). Replace the §5 keyboard paragraph (lines 78-82) with: "Keyboard: every pointer action is reachable by keyboard. A list is a `role="listbox"` whose rows are `role="option"` with roving `tabIndex` (one tab stop per list; the active row has `tabIndex=0`). ArrowUp/ArrowDown move, Home/End jump to the first and last row; moving changes the active row, not the selection. Enter activates the row (main panel: selects it and shows its details; sidebar: performs its primary action), Ctrl+Enter (Cmd+Enter on macOS) performs the secondary action, Delete (Cmd+Backspace on macOS) uninstalls where applicable, and Shift+F10 or the ContextMenu key opens the actions menu for the active row; right-click anywhere on the row opens the same menu. Row action buttons are pointer-only: they sit next to the option in a container hidden from assistive technology (`aria-hidden="true"`, `tabIndex={-1}`), become visible on `:hover` and `:focus-within` of the row, and clicking one keeps focus on the row. Each listbox describes its keys in a visually hidden sentence (`aria-describedby`); outcomes are announced through the one `role="status"` region (`status-live`). A row with a pending operation is `aria-busy="true"`, its status text is part of its description and a spinner replaces its actions. A disabled row (`aria-disabled="true"`, e.g. unresolved) keeps focus, selection and the context menu but ignores Enter, Ctrl+Enter and Delete. When the active row disappears, focus moves to the row now at its index (or the last one); when the list empties, the empty-state container takes focus." Note in the STATUS that Cmd+Backspace is an addition over 1.x (`SidebarApp.tsx:1142` handled `Delete` only) and that Space, PageUp/PageDown and multi-selection are reserved for P5. §8: change the `package-row-<id>` cell to "A row (id lower-cased): the wrapper around the option and its pointer actions; the option is the `role="option"` inside it (read `aria-selected` there)"; add `row-action-<name>` | "A pointer action button inside a row (`install`, `update`, `uninstall`, `more`); hidden from assistive technology, mirrored by the keys of §5"; add `section-header-<name>` | "The toggle button of a collapsible section (`installed`, `updates`)"; `status-live` and `sash` unchanged. §4 needs no edit: "Skeleton rows (3) + `role="status"` text" is the Skeleton plus the `LiveStatus` message.
11. `src/test/lint/webview-stylesheets.test.ts` (backend project; the folder is included): over every `.css` under `src/webview/{shared,main,sidebar-next}` except `tokens.css`: `no reduced-motion rule outside tokens.css`, `no !important`, `no px font size`, `every animation and transition duration is a duration token`, `every class selector starts with .nuiget-`, and for `primitives.css` specifically `row actions are shown on hover and focus-within of the row` (the two selectors of step 8 are present). Add this file to the item's files under this ruling.
12. Order and commits. Commit a: steps 1-6, 8 (the rules those primitives need), 9 (their tests), 10, 11, CHANGELOG `### Added` `**2.0 webview primitives** — …` (first version), tracker and STATUS; message `feat(webview/shared): listbox, row and the first primitives (P3.2, ADR-008, ADR-006, U1, U3, U7, U11)`. Commit b: step 7 and its tests, the composed test, `primitives/index.ts` (named re-exports of every primitive plus `export * from './icons'`, and `import './primitives.css'`), ARCHITECTURE and the gotcha rows, CHANGELOG entry completed; message `feat(webview/shared): section header, status, empty, skeleton, progress, banner and sash (P3.2, ADR-008, U1, U6, U11)`. Each commit passes `npx tsc --noEmit`, `npm run check-types`, `npx eslint src`, `npx vitest run --project frontend src/webview/shared`, `npx vitest run --project backend src/test/lint`, `npm test`, `npm run bench` before push; `package:vsix`, `check:vsix`, `docs:protocol:check`, `test:ui` SKIPPED with the literal reason (no host, packaging, protocol or UI file changed). If the session must end after commit a, the row is `in progress` with "Exact next step: P3.2 commit b" in both STATUS files; that is not a split and not a second row.
13. Docs in commit b. ARCHITECTURE.md: the `src/webview/shared/**` row (line 104) gains "primitives present since P3.2 (`primitives/index.ts`)", and a subsection "Primitives and the listbox contract" under the 2.0 section: the Row DOM sketch, why the actions are a hidden sibling, the keyboard table, how numbers reach the DOM (`cssVars`, custom properties, stylesheet owns the property), one stylesheet, the live region. Gotcha rows (2.0 table): "Buttons inside `role="option"`" → axe `nested-interactive` fails even at `tabIndex -1`; actions live in the `aria-hidden` sibling container and every one has a key in the listbox; "Attributes on the row wrapper" → nothing but `className`, `data-*` and the ref on the `role="presentation"` wrapper, or `aria-required-children` fails; "Inline style in 2.0 webviews" → only `cssVars()` with `--nuiget-*` properties, the stylesheet owns the real property, the tests read `style.cssText`; "`useVirtualizer` mocked in tests" → never in 2.0 tests, stub `getBoundingClientRect` instead; "New primitive without its stylesheet import" → each primitive module imports `primitives.css`. Ledger: U1 progressed (closes at P3.4), U3 progressed (static focus ring; closes P5.11), U6 progressed (`IconButton` requires a name; closes P5.1), U7 progressed, U11 progressed with the 24 px note, U12 progressed by the §5 rewrite. Coverage: run `npx vitest run --coverage`, paste the `src/webview/**` figure, leave `vitest.config.mts` untouched (P3.6 decides the ratchet).

DO NOT:
- Put a `button`, `tabIndex`, or any focusable element inside the `role="option"`, at any `tabIndex`.
- Put an `aria-*` attribute or `tabIndex` on the `role="presentation"` wrapper, or `aria-label` on the option, a `Badge` span or any role-less span.
- Use `aria-activedescendant`, `inert` on the actions (it blocks pointer events too), `aria-keyshortcuts` per option, or `display: none` for hidden actions.
- Handle Space, PageUp/PageDown, Left/Right, Escape or type-ahead in the `Listbox`, or add `aria-multiselectable` now.
- Mock `useVirtualizer` or `@tanstack/react-virtual` in any 2.0 test; stub `getBoundingClientRect` and let the real virtualizer run.
- Render a layout property (`height`, `transform`, `width`, `top`) through the `style` prop; only `--nuiget-*` custom properties through `cssVars`.
- Add a token for the spin duration, a `prefers-reduced-motion` or `forced-colors` block, a colour literal or `!important` to `primitives.css`.
- Give `Skeleton`, `EmptyState` or `Banner` the test id `status-live`, or make `Skeleton` a live region; keep `LiveStatus` the one `status-live`.
- Put `tabIndex` on `EmptyState`; the Listbox's empty container is the focus target.
- Mutate `document.body.style` or add document-level mouse listeners in `Sash`; use pointer capture.
- Create tracker rows `P3.2a`/`P3.2b`, change `vitest.config.mts`, `eslint.config.mjs`, `esbuild.mjs`, `package.json`, CI, `src/protocol/**`, `src/core/contracts.ts` or `docs/adr/**`; import `tokens.css` from any entry (P3.4).
- Edit interaction-model §4, or any 1.x component or test.

CONTRACT CHANGE: none. `src/protocol/**`, `src/core/contracts.ts` and `docs/adr/**` stay as they are (ADR-008:29-32 lists primitives as a floor; `Listbox`, `Spinner`, `ProgressBar` and `Banner` are the plan row's additions); `.claude/.contract-change` must not exist. `docs/ux/interaction-model.md` is not frozen and the row names it. Approved as files the row does not name: `src/test/lint/webview-stylesheets.test.ts` (new), one assertion list in `src/test/lint/webview-tokens.test.ts` only if a token is added (none is), `src/webview/shared/primitives/cssVars.ts`, `primitives.css`, `primitives/index.ts`, `composed.test.tsx`.

VERIFY: `npx vitest run --project frontend src/webview/shared` green with the titles of step 9, including the three negative axe tests and the composed test; `npx vitest run --project backend src/test/lint` green with the six titles of step 11; `grep -c 'role="presentation"' src/webview/shared/primitives/Row.tsx` → 1 and `grep -nE '<button|tabIndex' src/webview/shared/primitives/Row.tsx` shows no button and no tabIndex inside the option markup; `grep -rn "style={{" src/webview/shared/primitives/*.tsx` → 0 lines (every style goes through `cssVars`); `grep -rn "vi.mock('@tanstack" src/webview/shared` → 0; `grep -c 'prefers-reduced-motion' src/webview/shared/primitives/primitives.css` → 0 and `grep -c '!important' …/primitives.css` → 0; `grep -n 'row-height-compact' src/webview/shared/tokens.css` shows `24px`; `grep -n 'row-action-<name>\|section-header-<name>\|Cmd+Backspace' docs/ux/interaction-model.md` → three hits; `npx tsc --noEmit && npm run check-types && npx eslint src` → 0 each; `npm test` and `npm run bench` green; `git log --oneline -2` shows the two P3.2 commits, each with a green CI run; `test -e .claude/.contract-change` → false; `git diff --stat labs/2.0@{upstream}~2 -- vitest.config.mts eslint.config.mjs esbuild.mjs package.json src/protocol src/core docs/adr` → empty.

FILES CHECKED: `docs/plans/2026-10-11-nuiget-2.0-execution-plan.md:41-175,240-251,270-286,208`; `docs/plans/STATUS.md:21-82,431,1264-1356` and its phase-review headings; `docs/plans/findings-ledger.md:76-87,245-256,307-311`; `docs/ux/interaction-model.md` (whole); `docs/adr/006-icons-and-csp.md`, `docs/adr/008-webview-architecture.md` (whole); `src/webview/shared/STATUS.md` (whole), `tokens.css` (whole), `primitives/icons.test.tsx` (whole); `src/test/setup-frontend.ts`; `src/test/lint/webview-tokens.test.ts`, `webview-colours.test.ts` (whole); `vitest.config.mts:1-80`; `eslint.config.mjs:1-80`; `package.json:310-360`; `ARCHITECTURE.md:104,160-192`; `CHANGELOG.md:1-25`; `.github/instructions/a11y.instructions.md:1-120,237-243,297-304,484-493,665,724-738`; `scripts/hooks/guard.mjs:4-16,67-81,167`; `src/webview/sidebar/components/PackageRow.tsx`, `SectionHeader.tsx`, `src/webview/app/components/DraggableSash.tsx` (whole); `src/webview/app/App.tsx:1803-1807,3025-3060`; `src/webview/app/components/InstalledTab.test.tsx:1-11`; `src/webview/sidebar/SidebarApp.tsx:1132-1146`; `src/webview/main/`, `src/webview/sidebar-next/` listings; `node_modules/axe-core/axe.js` (`getOwnedRoles` at 27366, `ariaRequiredChildrenEvaluate`, `getMissingContext`, `noFocusableContentEvaluate`, `getFocusableDescendants`, `usesUnreliableHidingStrategy`, `focusableNotTabbableEvaluate`, `ariaRequiredAttrEvaluate`, the `option`, `listbox`, `separator`, `progressbar`, `status`, `presentation` role entries; version 4.12.1); `node_modules/jsdom/lib/jsdom/living/css/CSSStyleDeclaration-impl.js:140,324,415,461` (jsdom 30.1.2); `node_modules/@tanstack/react-virtual/dist/esm/index.d.ts:2,48`, `node_modules/@tanstack/virtual-core/dist/esm/index.d.ts:36,63,72,74`, `.../index.js` (`observeElementRect`, `measureElement`, `calculateRangeImpl`, `scrollWithAdjustments`; 3.14.14 / 3.18.0); `git log --oneline -60` (clean at `3794311`; multi-commit precedents P0.13, P1.3, P1.6). Not checked (outside the repository): the quarry's `STATUS.md`, taken from the brief's excerpt; its claim about `nested-interactive` is confirmed by the axe source, its plan to set custom properties in effects is replaced by the `style` prop, which ADR-006 names. The ambient advisor was consulted twice and agreed with the decision; the four points where this ruling differs from its suggestions (one stylesheet, the test id on the wrapper, the empty container as focus target, the 24 px compact row) are recorded above with their reasons.
`````

### P3.5 — stop-and-ask: putting a 2.0 build under the e2e and ExTester suites (2026-10-13)

Brief: plan §2.4 form, item P3.5, on `labs/2.0` @ `8c95e9e` with nothing written. The row names two test files
and `ci.yml`. In the tree, `.vscode-test.mjs` has one configuration whose glob would run a new `v2-*` e2e file
in the existing 1.x `E2E` jobs; `vsce` and ExTester always run `vscode:prepublish`, which builds the 1.x
entries over whatever `--v2` wrote, so no existing script yields a 2.0 VSIX; `check:vsix` requires
`dist/webview.css`, which a `--v2` build does not have (N62); the fixture project is shared with the 1.x
suites; an extension-host test cannot see the webview. It asked which files beyond the three may change and by
which mechanism the two jobs get the 2.0 entries, what each suite asserts, which fixture, what happens to
`check:vsix`, the shape of the jobs and how a red run of a new job counts. Answer, verbatim; no sentinel, no
frozen path:

`````text
DECISION: Option 1, narrowed: one environment switch, `NUIGET_V2=1`, read by `esbuild.mjs` and by `.vscode-test.mjs`; one new npm script (`test:ui:v2`; the env switch makes `npm run test:e2e` select the v2 suite, so no e2e script is added); the two new CI jobs set the variable at job level; the shared fixture is kept and restored; `check:vsix` is not run on a 2.0 build. Not `OWNER MUST DECIDE`: every file this touches is tooling, and `package.json` `contributes` stays untouched (§2.3).

WHY: The brief is right about the tree. `vsce` always runs `vscode:prepublish` (`node_modules/@vscode/vsce/out/package.js:1561`, unconditional in `packageCommand`; `createVSIX` at `api.js:25` calls the same function, so ExTester's `--package_options` cannot skip it), and the prepublish child is spawned with `shell: true` and no `env` override (`package.js:1477`), so an environment variable is the one switch that reaches `npm run package` → `node esbuild.mjs --production` inside both `npx vsce package` and `extest setup-and-run`. `.vscode-test.mjs` has exactly one configuration and already reads `process.env.VSCODE_TEST_VERSION` (`.vscode-test.mjs:7`), so an env-selected `files` pattern follows the file's own precedent; `@vscode/test-cli` expands `files` with `glob()` and supports no negation (`node_modules/@vscode/test-cli/out/cli/gatherFiles.mjs:10-18`), so the legacy pattern must exclude `v2-*` by extglob. Option 2 (hand-assembled extension folder) bypasses `.vscodeignore` and is a second packaging path to maintain; option 3 leaves the P3 gate's "usable for installed + uninstall" unproven, because only ExTester can reach the webview (`src/extension.next.ts:41` exports nothing, no command uninstalls). Plan §2.1 is read as: the row names `ci.yml`, and the configuration that `ci.yml`'s new jobs cannot work without (`esbuild.mjs:7`, `.vscode-test.mjs`, `package.json` `scripts`) is in scope by this ruling; nothing else is. Finding N62 already rules that the v2 jobs do not run `check:vsix`; N44 already assigns "bounded polling, no fixed sleeps" to the P3.5 e2e test.

DO:
1. `esbuild.mjs:7`: `const isV2 = process.argv.includes('--v2') || process.env.NUIGET_V2 === '1';` with a one-line comment: the variable exists so that `vscode:prepublish`, run by `vsce` and ExTester, builds the 2.0 entries in the `e2e-v2`/`ui-v2` jobs. No other change to the file; `--v2` stays the documented flag (D4).
2. `.vscode-test.mjs`: `const v2 = process.env.NUIGET_V2 === '1';` and `files: v2 ? 'out/test/e2e/v2-*.e2e.test.js' : 'out/test/e2e/!(v2-*).e2e.test.js'`. Everything else (version, workspaceFolder, mocha, launchArgs) unchanged. Verify the extglob before relying on it: after `npx tsc -p tsconfig.test.json`, `node -e "import('glob').then(g=>g.glob('out/test/e2e/!(v2-*).e2e.test.js').then(f=>console.log(f.length,f)))"` → 9 files, none starting with `v2-`; the same with `'out/test/e2e/v2-*.e2e.test.js'` → exactly the new file.
3. `package.json` scripts: add `"test:ui:v2": "tsc -p tsconfig.test.json && extest setup-and-run out/test/ui/v2/*.ui.test.js --code_version max --code_settings src/test/ui/settings.json --open_resource src/test/e2e/fixtures/e2e-workspace"`. It does not call `package:vsix` (that reruns `npm ci` and the whole `npm test`; ExTester packages the VSIX itself, and under `NUIGET_V2=1` that VSIX is the 2.0 one). Do not change `test:e2e`, `test:ui`, `package`, `vscode:prepublish`. No new dependency. `.claude/settings.json` needs no change: `Bash(npm run test:*)` and `Bash(xvfb-run -a npm run test:*)` already cover both suites; an env-prefixed invocation that prompts is run anyway and the prompt noted (§2.2 step 5).
4. `src/test/e2e/v2-sidebar.e2e.test.ts` (the row's path; Mocha `tdd` ui like its neighbours). Titles the gate reads:
`````

### P3.6 — ⚑ ruling: the re-based estimate, the order of P4 and P5, and five handed decisions (2026-10-14)

Brief: plan §2.4 form, item P3.6, on `labs/2.0` @ `3b6f987` with nothing written. It pasted the measured effort
per phase and per item (commit-to-commit times from `git log`, the owner gap removed, CI wait included), the rows
that remain by type, the quarry's `src/core/http` and `src/core/feeds` (line counts, test counts, the stale
`STATUS.md` claims, quoted), the ordering hooks (18 `REMOVAL GATE` comments, N61, N62, D5, the harness that drives
the client model and not the slices) and the five decisions earlier rows handed to P3.6 (N63, N61, N64, the compact
row, the `src/webview/**` coverage ratchet), with three estimate options and four ordering options, unpicked. It
asked for the re-based estimate with its unit, the ordering and row splits, and the ruling on each handed decision
with the item and files that carry it. Answer, verbatim; no sentinel, no frozen path:

`````text
DECISION: Re-base in measured hours, not session-days; reorder so that P5.1, P5.3 and P5.4 run before P4.1 and P4 then runs whole; dissolve P5.2 into the feature rows (the row stays as a closing sweep, never `skipped`); split P5.12 into P5.12a (e2e ports) and P5.12b (ExTester gap-fill, retirement map, CI shape); from P5.4 on every feature row adds its own ExTester test. Handed decisions: N63 → both measures, `/* @__PURE__ */` on the icons in P5.1 and a 2.0 ceiling table in `esbuild.mjs` written by P3.6 (sidebar 329 122 = the 1.x ceiling 304 097 + the virtualizer's measured 25 025; extension and webview unchanged); N61 → P5.4 moves the queued state into `src/webview/shared/store/ops.tsx` and deletes the sidebar's pending set; N64 → no ExTester job (its oldest VS Code is 1.138.0), a CSS-baseline lint in P5.11, the 1.85 run is the owner's in P7.4; compact row → keep 24 px, no file changes; ratchet → `src/webview/**` stays 62/52/54/64 and P3.6 adds `'src/webview/{shared,main,sidebar-next}/**': { lines: 85 }` to `vitest.config.mts`. Files of P3.6 under this ruling: `docs/plans/STATUS.md`, plan §7 (`docs/plans/2026-10-11-nuiget-2.0-execution-plan.md:329-342` only), `docs/plans/findings-ledger.md` rows N61, N63, N64, U11 and the P3.5 note of N62, `esbuild.mjs` (the ceiling table and `reportCeilings` only), `vitest.config.mts` (one threshold key), one gotcha row in `.github/copilot-instructions.md`, one sentence in the ARCHITECTURE.md 2.0 tooling subsection. Not `OWNER MUST DECIDE`: the row's own text is "adjust P4/P5 ordering", the ledger hands N63 and N64 to P3.6, and nothing frozen or under `contributes` moves.

WHY: The brief is right about the tree, with one correction that changes the weight of N63: no CI job runs `--analyze` (`.github/workflows/ci.yml:164,278,383` are plain `--production`), and `esbuild.mjs:146-157` checks `BUNDLE_CEILINGS` only under `--analyze --production`, so the ceiling is a local gate until P7.2 ("bundle ceilings enforced") and N63 cannot turn a CI run red; it can only fail the implementing session's own measurement, which is why the 2.0 table must exist before the sidebar grows. The 1.x sidebar does not bundle `@tanstack/react-virtual` (`grep -rln react-virtual src/webview/sidebar src/webview/app` lists `src/webview/app/**` only), so the 2.0 sidebar carries 25 025 bytes the 1.x ceiling never budgeted; adding exactly that share is a derivation P7.2 can recompute, not a guess. The 26 `React.memo(` calls at `src/webview/shared/primitives/icons.tsx:27-239` are kept by the bundler because an unannotated call may have side effects; `/* @__PURE__ */` lets esbuild drop the unused ones (ESM input, tree shaking is on when bundling, output format is irrelevant). The measured effort is verified against `git log --reverse --format='%at %h %s' origin/main..HEAD`: P0 08:34→12:53 +0200, P1 →17:26, P2 →21:38 plus 18:47→22:30 the next day, P3 22:30→02:26, ≈ 20.7 h for 40 planned session-days, i.e. 0.52 h per planned day, with the P2.10 root (61 min) and P2.11 harness (74 min) the largest items; the by-type projection is used because P5 features are the most expensive measured type and the per-feature ExTester tests this ruling adds were not in the plan's days. D5 ("sidebar-first vertical slice, then the main panel; backend built strictly in slice order") is the reason for the reorder, not an obstacle to it: P4 as written is ten backend rows that no surface exercises until P5.5, the pattern D5's rationale names, and the three pulled rows read no feed (P5.1 and P5.3 by their row text; P5.4's `installed/get`, `transitive/get`, `allProjects/installed`, `ops/remove` and `ops/bulkRemove` are answered for real at `src/host/handlers.ts:270,286,375,379` over `Catalog.ts:119,192,212`, with only `transitive/metadata` at `handlers.ts:283` hitting the gate at `Catalog.ts:197`). The slice-with-first-consumer rule comes from P3.3/P3.4: the one store gap of the slice (N61) was found by the first consumer, and interaction model §4 ("Pending op: from `ops/*` `queued`/`running` chunks until the terminal outcome") already says the pending state belongs to the slice, which `ops.tsx:114-120` ignores (`run()` passes no `onChunk`). ExTester 8.28.1 declares `supportedVersions.vscode-min: 1.138.0, vscode-max: 1.140.0` (`node_modules/vscode-extension-tester/package.json:59-61`), so a `--code_version min` job would run a Chromium far newer than 114 and prove nothing; `activate` returns `void` (`src/extension.next.ts:41`), so an extension-host test on 1.85.0 cannot observe the webview through `exports` either. The compact row stays at 24 px because P5.11's own gate reads "≥ 24 px targets" and the ExTester layout test reads the token's value from `tokens.css` (value-agnostic); 22 px would put a 24 px `IconButton` outside its row. Vitest resolves per-glob thresholds with picomatch (`node_modules/vitest/dist/chunks/index.DpLw24bj.js:53,15168`), which expands braces, so one key covers the three 2.0 directories (measured 99.42 %, 100 % and the placeholder with its test), and `lines: 85` mirrors the only shape of floor this repository has measured (`src/core/**`, `vitest.config.mts:49-51`). Precedents for naming config files in a ruling: P1.2 added the core threshold inside a code item; the P3.1 ruling named `package.json` and `tsconfig.vitest.json`; the P3.5 ruling named `esbuild.mjs`, `.vscode-test.mjs` and a script. Nothing parses the tracker (`grep -rln "plans/STATUS" src scripts` finds only STATUS notes and `scripts/hooks/guard.test.mjs:83`, which asserts the file is editable), and `BUNDLE_CEILINGS` is read only in `esbuild.mjs`, so the new row IDs and the keyed table break no test.
`````
